{\mathfrak{g}}=C_{N}^{(1)},A_{2N}^{(2)}
,
t_{1}=1
otherwise,

g^{({\mathfrak{g}})}\,H_{1}^{[m]}\,(g^{({\mathfrak{g}})})^{-1}=(g_{T}\,g_{\Lambda})^{t_{1}}\,H_{1}^{[m]}\,(g_{T}\,g_{\Lambda})^{-t_{1}}.

Proof.

The only subtleties arise in the cases
{\mathfrak{g}}=B_{N}^{(1)},A_{2N}^{(2)}
. When
{\mathfrak{g}}=B_{N}^{(1)}
, with
g=g_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\alpha}g_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}g_{\Lambda}\beta
, the action of
g_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}g_{\Lambda}\beta
on
H_{1}^{[m]}
creates a term proportional to
T_{m+1}
,
as a consequence of

(C.5)

g_{T}\,g_{\Lambda}\,\left(1-\frac{{\Lambda}_{a}}{{\Lambda}_{a-1}}\right)\,T_{a}=\left(T_{a}-\frac{{\Lambda}_{a+1}}{{\Lambda}_{a}}\,T_{a+1}\right)\,g_{T}g_{\Lambda}

for
a=m
.
This term commutes with
{\alpha}
only if
m+1\leq N-1
, hence we set
m_{\mathfrak{g}}=N-2
.
Similarly, when
{\mathfrak{g}}=A_{2N}^{(2)}
, with
g=g_{T}g_{\Lambda}{\alpha}g_{T}g_{\Lambda}\beta
, the action of

g_{T}g_{\Lambda}\beta
on
H_{1}^{[m]}
creates a term proportional to
T_{m+1}
which commutes with
{\alpha}
only if
m+1\leq N-1
, hence we set
m_{\mathfrak{g}}=N-2
as well.
∎

Lemma C.3
.

We have the commutation relations for
m=1,2,...,N-1
:

g_{T}\,g_{\Lambda}\,H_{1}^{[m]}=\left\{H_{1}^{[m]}+\frac{{\Lambda}_{m+1}}{{\Lambda}_{m}}\,\left(\frac{1}{T_{m}}-T_{m+1}\right)\right\}\,g_{T}\,g_{\Lambda}.

Proof.

We simply sum over the relevant values of
a
the commutation relations (
C.5
), as well as the following:

(C.6)

g_{T}\,g_{\Lambda}\,\left(1-\frac{{\Lambda}_{a+1}}{{\Lambda}_{a}}\right)\,\frac{1}{T_{a}}=\left(\frac{1}{T_{a}}-\frac{{\Lambda}_{a}}{{\Lambda}_{a-1}}\,\frac{1}{T_{a-1}}\right)\,g_{T}g_{\Lambda},

obtained by combining (
C.2
-
C.4
).
∎

The cases
{\mathfrak{g}}=C_{N}^{(1)}
and
A_{2N}^{(1)}
are special, as we will have to use Lemma
C.3

twice
. This is deferred to Section
C.6
below.

For all the other cases, we apply Lemmas
C.2
and
C.3
to commute
g
through the relevant bulk contributions to
H_{1}^{({\mathfrak{g}})}
corresponding to
a=1,2,..,m_{\mathfrak{g}}
. The remaining terms of
H_{1}^{({\mathfrak{g}})}
are the “boundary” terms, corresponding to
m_{\mathfrak{g}}<a\leq N
and possibly to constant terms independent of
T_{a}^{\pm 1}
. We address them individually in the following Sections
C.2
through
C.5
. In all cases, the Lemma
4.10
follows from summing all contributions
from the bulk
a=1,2,...,m_{\mathfrak{g}}
and the boundary
a=m_{\mathfrak{g}}+1,...,N
plus constant terms.

C.2.
Case
{\mathfrak{g}}=D_{N}^{(1)}

Recall that
g=g_{T}g_{\Lambda}\prod_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(1-\frac{q^{n}}{{\Lambda}_{N-1}{\Lambda}_{N}}\right)^{-1}
, and that
m_{\mathfrak{g}}=N-2
.
For
a=N-1
, we have:

\displaystyle g\left(1-\frac{{\Lambda}_{N-1}}{{\Lambda}_{N-2}}\right)T_{N-1}=\left(T_{N-1}-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\,T_{N}-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N-1}\Lambda_{N}}T_{N}^{-1}+\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N-1}^{2}}T_{N-1}^{-1}\right)\,g,

\displaystyle g\,\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N-1}\Lambda_{N}}\right)T_{N-1}^{-1}=\left(T_{N-1}^{-1}-\frac{\Lambda_{N-1}}{\Lambda_{N-2}}\,T_{N-2}^{-1}\right)\,g,

and for
a=N
:

\displaystyle g\,\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)T_{N}=\left(T_{N}-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N-1}\Lambda_{N}}T_{N-1}^{-1}\right)\,g,

\displaystyle g\,\left(1-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N-1}\Lambda_{N}}\right)\,T_{N}^{-1}=\left(T_{N}^{-1}-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\,T_{N-1}^{-1}\right)\,g.

Eq. (
C.1
) follows by summing all bulk and boundary contributions.

C.3.
Case
{\mathfrak{g}}=B_{N}^{(1)}

Recall that
g=g_{1}\,g_{2}
,
g_{1}=g_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\alpha}
,
g_{2}=g_{1}g_{\Lambda}
with
{\alpha}=\prod_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(1-\frac{q^{n}}{{\Lambda}_{N}^{2}}\right)^{-1}
, and that
m_{\mathfrak{g}}=N-2
.
The terms
a=N-1,N
and the constant term read

\displaystyle g\,\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)\,T_{N-1}^{-1}=T_{N-1}^{-1}\,g_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\,{\alpha}g_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\,\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N-1}}{\Lambda_{N-2}}\right){\alpha}\,g_{\Lambda}=\left(T_{N-1}^{-1}-\frac{\Lambda_{N-1}}{\Lambda_{N-2}}T_{N-2}^{-1}\right)\,g,

\displaystyle g\,\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N-1}}{\Lambda_{N-2}}\right)\,T_{N-1}=T_{N-1}\,g_{1}g_{T}^{\frac{1}{2}}\,\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)\,{\alpha}g_{\Lambda}=g_{1}\,\left(T_{N-1}-q^{-1}\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}T_{N}^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{N-1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\,g_{2},

\displaystyle g\,\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)\,T_{N}=g_{1}\,\left(1-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}T_{N}^{-1}\right)\left(1-q^{-2}\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}T_{N}^{-1}\right)\,T_{N}\,g_{2},

\displaystyle g\,\left(1-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}\right)\left(1-q^{2}\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}\right)\,T_{N}^{-1}=g_{1}\,\left(T_{N}^{-1}-q^{-1}\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}T_{N-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T_{N}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\,g,

\displaystyle g\,\left(\frac{q^{-2}}{\Lambda_{N-1}\Lambda_{N}}-\frac{1+q^{-2}}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}\right)=g_{1}\left(\frac{q^{-1}}{\Lambda_{N-1}\Lambda_{N}}T_{N-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}T_{N}^{-\frac{1}{2}}-q^{2}\frac{1+q^{-2}}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}T_{N}^{-1}\right)\,g_{2}.

Summing these five terms gives

\displaystyle g\left\{\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)\,T_{N-1}^{-1}+\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N-1}}{\Lambda_{N-2}}\right)\,T_{N-1}+\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)\,T_{N}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left.\left(1-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}\right)\left(1-q^{2}\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}\right)\,T_{N}^{-1}+\frac{q^{-2}}{\Lambda_{N-1}\Lambda_{N}}-\frac{1+q^{-2}}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\left\{T_{N-1}+\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)T_{N}-\frac{{\Lambda}_{N-1}}{{\Lambda}_{N-2}}T_{N-2}^{-1}+\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)T_{N-1}^{-1}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left.\left(1-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}\right)\left(1-q^{2}\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}\right)\,T_{N}^{-1}+\frac{q^{-2}}{\Lambda_{N-1}\Lambda_{N}}-\frac{1+q^{-2}}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}\right\}\,g.

Eq. (
C.1
) follows by summing all bulk and boundary contributions.

C.4.
Case
{\mathfrak{g}}=A_{2N-1}^{(2)}

Recall that
g=g_{T}g_{\Lambda}{\alpha}
, with
{\alpha}=\prod_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(1-\frac{q^{2n}}{{\Lambda}_{N}^{2}}\right)^{-1}
, and
m_{\mathfrak{g}}=N-1
. For the two boundary terms for
a=N
we have:

\displaystyle g\,\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)T_{N}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{T}\,\left(1-q^{-2}\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}\right)T_{N}\,g_{\Lambda}\,{\alpha}=\left(T_{N}-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}T_{N}^{-1}\right)\,g,

\displaystyle g\,\left(1-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}\right)T_{N}^{-1}\,g^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{T}\,\left(1-q^{-1}\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)T_{N}^{-1}\,g_{\Lambda}\,{\alpha}=\left(T_{N}^{-1}-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}T_{N-1}^{-1}\right)\,g.

Eq. (
C.1
) follows by summing all bulk and boundary contributions.

C.5.
Case
{\mathfrak{g}}=D_{N+1}^{(2)}

Recall that
g=g_{T}g_{\Lambda}{\alpha}
, with
{\alpha}=\prod_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(1-\frac{q^{\frac{n}{2}}}{{\Lambda}_{N}}\right)^{-1}
, and
m_{\mathfrak{g}}=N-1
. For the two boundary terms for
a=N
and the constant term we have:

\displaystyle g\,\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)T_{N}=\left(T_{N}-\frac{1+q^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{\Lambda_{N}}+\frac{q}{\Lambda_{N}^{2}}T_{N}^{-1}\right)\,g,

\displaystyle g\,\left(1-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}}\right)\left(1-\frac{q^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\Lambda_{N}}\right)T_{N}^{-1}=g_{T}\,\left(1-q^{-1}\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)T_{N}^{-1}\,g_{\Lambda}\,{\alpha}=\left(T_{N}^{-1}-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}T_{N-1}^{-1}\right)\,g,

\displaystyle g\,\left(-\frac{1+q^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{\Lambda_{N}}\right)=-\frac{1+q^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\Lambda_{N}}T_{N}^{-1}\,g.

Eq. (
C.1
) follows by summing all bulk and boundary contributions.

C.6.
Cases
{\mathfrak{g}}=C_{N}^{(1)},A_{2N}^{(2)}

Recall the general structure of the operator
g=g_{1}g_{2}
with
g_{1}=g_{T}g_{\Lambda}{\alpha}
and
g_{2}=g_{T}g_{\Lambda}\beta
, where
{\alpha},\beta
are functions of
{\Lambda}_{N}
only. We note that
g_{2}
is the
same
for both cases, with
\beta=\prod_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(1-\frac{q^{n}}{{\Lambda}_{N}}\right)^{-1}
. We note also that
H_{1}^{[N-1]}
is the same for both cases, and Lemma
C.3
gives the commutation of
g_{2}
through
H_{1}^{[N-1]}
:

(C.7)

g_{2}\,H_{1}^{[N-1]}=\left\{H_{1}^{[N-1]}+\frac{{\Lambda}_{N}}{{\Lambda}_{N-1}}\,\left(\frac{1}{T_{N-1}}-T_{N}\right)\right\}\,g_{2}.

We now need to add the boundary and constant terms.
For
{\mathfrak{g}}=C_{N}^{(1)}
we have for
a=N
(and no constant term):

\displaystyle g_{2}\,\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)T_{N}=\left(T_{N}-\frac{q^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{\Lambda_{N}}\right)\,g_{2},\ \ g_{2}\,\left(1-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}}\right)T_{N}^{-1}=\left(T_{N}^{-1}-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}T_{N-1}^{-1}\right)\,g_{2}.

For
{\mathfrak{g}}=A_{2N}^{(2)}
we have for
a=N
and the constant term:

\displaystyle g_{2}\,\left(1-\frac{{\Lambda}_{N}}{{\Lambda}_{N-1}}\right)T_{N}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{T}\,\left(1-\frac{q^{-1}}{{\Lambda}_{N}}\right)T_{N}\,g_{\Lambda}\,\beta=\left(T_{N}-\frac{q^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{{\Lambda}_{N}}\right)\,g_{2},

\displaystyle g_{2}\,\left(1-\frac{1}{{\Lambda}_{N}}\right)T_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(T_{N}^{-1}-\frac{{\Lambda}_{N}}{{\Lambda}_{N-1}}T_{N-1}^{-1}\right)\,g_{2},

\displaystyle g_{2}\,\left(1-\frac{1}{{\Lambda}_{N}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{q^{\frac{1}{2}}}{{\Lambda}_{N}}T_{N}^{-1}\right)\,g_{2}.

Adding these to the bulk contributions (
C.7
), we deduce the following commutations:

(C.8)

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{g}}=C_{N}^{(1)}:

\displaystyle g_{2}H_{1}=\left\{H_{1}+\frac{1}{{\Lambda}_{N}}(T_{N}^{-1}-q^{-\frac{1}{2}})\right\}g_{2},

(C.9)

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{g}}=A_{2N}^{(2)}:

\displaystyle g_{2}H_{1}=\left\{H_{1}+\frac{1-q^{\frac{1}{2}}}{{\Lambda}_{N}}(T_{N}^{-1}-q^{-\frac{1}{2}})\right\}g_{2}.

We must now commute
g_{1}
through this. For both cases, we split again
H_{1}
into a bulk piece
H_{1}^{[N-1]}

and boundary pieces and constant terms. For the bulk contribution, we use again Lemma
C.3
to write in both cases

(C.10)

g_{1}\,H_{1}^{[N-1]}=\left\{H_{1}^{[N-1]}+\frac{{\Lambda}_{N}}{{\Lambda}_{N-1}}\,\left(\frac{1}{T_{N-1}}-T_{N}\right)\right\}\,g_{1}.

Then for
{\mathfrak{g}}=C_{N}^{(1)}
and
a=N
:

\displaystyle g_{1}\,\left(1-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}\right)T_{N}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{N}\,g_{1},

\displaystyle g_{1}\,\left(1-\frac{1}{\Lambda_{N}}\right)T_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{q^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\Lambda_{N}}T_{N}^{-1}\right)\left(T_{N}^{-1}-\frac{\Lambda_{N}}{\Lambda_{N-1}}T_{N-1}^{-1}\right)\,g_{1},

and for
{\mathfrak{g}}=A_{2N}^{(2)}
,
a=N
and the constant term:

\displaystyle g_{1}\,\left(1-\frac{{\Lambda}_{N}}{{\Lambda}_{N-1}}\right)T_{N}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g_{T}\,\left(1-\frac{q^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{{\Lambda}_{N}}\right)T_{N}\,g_{\Lambda}\,{\alpha}=\left(T_{N}-\frac{1}{{\Lambda}_{N}}\right)\,g_{1},

\displaystyle g_{1}\,\left(1-\frac{q^{\frac{1}{2}}}{{\Lambda}_{N}}\right)T_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(T_{N}^{-1}-\frac{{\Lambda}_{N}}{{\Lambda}_{N-1}}T_{N-1}^{-1}\right)\,g_{1},

\displaystyle g_{1}\,\left(1-\frac{q^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{{\Lambda}_{N}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{1}{{\Lambda}_{N}}T_{N}^{-1}\right)\,g_{1}.

Summing these with the bulk contributions (
C.8
)and (
C.9
) respectively, we arrive at

(C.11)

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{g}}=C_{N}^{(1)}:

\displaystyle g_{1}\,\left\{H_{1}+\frac{1}{{\Lambda}_{N}}(T_{N}^{-1}-q^{-\frac{1}{2}})\right\}=H_{1}\,g_{1},

(C.12)

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{g}}=A_{2N}^{(2)}:

\displaystyle g_{1}\,\left\{H_{1}+\frac{1-q^{\frac{1}{2}}}{{\Lambda}_{N}}(T_{N}^{-1}-q^{-\frac{1}{2}})\right\}=H_{1}\,g_{1}.

Finally, Lemma
4.10
follows for
{\mathfrak{g}}=C_{N}^{(1)},A_{2N}^{(2)}
by combining the commutation relations
(
C.8
-
C.9
) and (
C.11
-
C.12
). In all cases, Eq. (
C.1
) follows by summing all bulk and boundary contributions.
